public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {

        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {

                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}

    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;

    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j] ==> \result == -1;
    //@ ensures \result == -1 ==> (\forall int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);  
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k <= i; \forall int j; k + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[j];

        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k < i; arr[k] != arr[j];

        return -1;

    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;

    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j] ==> \result == -1;
    //@ ensures \result == -1 ==> (\forall int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);  
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 - 1 <= k <= i; \forall int j; k + 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[j];  
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;

public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; (\exists int j; i + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[i] == arr[j])) ==> \result != -1;  
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j] ==> \result == -1;
    //@ ensures \result == -1 ==> (\forall int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);  
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining \exists int k; 0 < k < i; \forall int j; k + 1 - 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[j];  
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k < i; arr[k] != arr[j];  
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }

public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; (\exists int j; i + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[i] == arr[j])) ==> \result != -1;  
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j] ==> \result == -1;
    //@ ensures \result == -1 ==> (\forall int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);  
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining \exists int k; 0 <= k < i; \exists int j; k + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[j];  
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k < i; arr[k] != arr[j];  
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}

public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; (\exists int j; i + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[i] == arr[j])) ==> \result != -1;  
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j] ==> \result == -1;
    //@ ensures \result == -1 ==> (\forall int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);  
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining \exists int k; 0 <= k <= i; \exists int j; k + 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[j];  
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k < i; arr[k] != arr[j];  
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}

public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; (\exists int j; i + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[i] == arr[j])) ==> \result != -1;  
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j] ==> \result == -1;
    //@ ensures \result == -1 ==> (\forall int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);  
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining \exists int k; 0 - 1 <= k < i; \exists int j; k + 1 - 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[j];  
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k < i; arr[k] != arr[j];  
            //@ loop_writes j;

                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }

public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; (\exists int j; i + 1 <= j <= arr.length; arr[i] == arr[j])) ==> \result != -1;  
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 - 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j] ==> \result == -1;  
    //@ ensures \result == -1 ==> (\forall int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);  
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
 
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
 
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}

public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i; 0 - 1 <= i < arr.length - 1; (\exists int j; i + 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[i] == arr[j])) ==> \result != -1;  
    //@ ensures \exists int i; 0 < i <= arr.length - 1; \exists int j; i + 1 < j <= arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j] ==> \result == -1;  
    //@ ensures \result == -1 ==> (\forall int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 < j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);  
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
 
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
 
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}

public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1; (\exists int j; i + 1 < j <= arr.length; arr[i] == arr[j])) ==> \result != -1;  
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 < i < arr.length - 1; \exists int j; i + 1 - 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j] ==> \result == -1;  
    //@ ensures \result == -1 ==> (\forall int i; 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 <= j <= arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);  
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
 
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
 
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}

    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
 
    //@ ensures \forall int i; 0 < i <= arr.length - 1; \exists int j; i + 1 - 1 <= j < arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j] ==> \result == -1;  
    //@ ensures \result == -1 ==> (\exists int i; 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1; \forall int j; i + 1 - 1 <= j <= arr.length; arr[i] != arr[j]);  
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
 
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
 
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}
